# Linux — inferred

The **7,726** projects that probably run on Linux but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [2,962 confirmed ones](linux.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← Linux](linux.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **2** of 11, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. Snapshot 2026-11-18. Stars, language, licence and last-push come from the GitHub API and drift daily.

The same data with screenshots embedded, filterable, is in the workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/latest/download/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/latest/download/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx).
